# Full Setup Navigation Tree WITH DESCRIPTIONS (Advanced FormSet 28.78)
# • = setting, ▸ = section, (0x..) = FormId

Advanced  (0x1)
    ▸  
    • Firmware Configuration  [oneof]
        meta: Setup @ 0xA (QID 0x16, 8b)
        help: Firmware Configuration options. NOTE:Ignore Policy Update(STR_FW_CONFIG_DEFAULT_VALUE) is to skip policy
        help: update and will ONLY WORK ON A PLATFORM.

    Debug Settings  (0x1006)
        ▸ Debug Settings
        ▸ 
        • Kernel Debug Serial Port  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x8 (QID 0x2C, 8b)
            help: Select Kernel Debug Port and report in ACPI DBG2 table
        • Serial Io Uart Debug Power Gating  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x786 (QID 0x2D, 8b)
            help: For S0iX support with Kernel Debugger Enabled. BIOS needs to change DBG2 Port Sub Type as value of 0x14
            help: (0x0014 Intel LPSS) Note: Requires OS support
        • Kernel Debug Patch  [oneof]
            meta: Setup @ 0x845 (QID 0x2E, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable Kernel Debug Patch
        • Platform Debug Consent  [oneof]
            meta: SiSetup @ 0x1 (QID 0x1007, 8b)
            help: Enabled(All Probes+TraceHub) supports all probes with TraceHub enabled and blocks s0ix  Enabled(Low Power)
            help: Tracehub is powergated by default, s0ix is viable  Manual:user needs to configure Advanced Debug Settings
            help: manually, aimed at advanced users
        VT-d Debug Settings  (0x155E)
            • IGD VTD Enable  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x88 (QID 0x31, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable IGD VTD
            • IPU VTD Enable  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x89 (QID 0x32, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable IPU VTD
            • IOP VTD Enable  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x8A (QID 0x33, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable IOP VTD
        Advanced Debug Settings  (0x1056)
            • USB3 Type-C UFP2DFP Kernel/Platform Debug Support  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0xA (QID 0x34, 8b)
                help: This BIOS option enables kernel and platform debug for USB3 interface over a UFP Type-C receptacle, select
                help: 'No Change' will do nothing to UFP2DFP setting
            • USB DbC Enable Mode  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x73F (QID 0x35, 8b)
                help: Disabled:Clear both USB2/3DBCEN USB2:Set USB2DBCEN USB3:Set USB3DBCEN Both:Set both USB2/3DBCEN No
                help: Change:Comply with HW value
            • DCI Enable  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x73E (QID 0x36, 8b)
                help: Determine if to enable DCI debug from host
            • PCH Trace Hub Enable Mode  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x714 (QID 0x37, 8b)
                help: Select 'Host Debugger' if Trace Hub is used with host debugger tool or 'Target Debugger' if Trace Hub is used
                help: by target debugger software. Note: If 'Host Debugger' is selected, Platform Debug Consent has to be ENABLED
                help: because DCI is one of the primary trace data output paths.
            • PCH TH Mem Buffer Size 0  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x715 (QID 0x38, 8b)
                help: Select size of memory region 0 buffer. Choose None/OS if OS-supported memory or trace forwarding is desired
                help: Note : Limit of total buffer size (PCH + CPU) is 512MB when iTBT disabled, 256MB when iTBT enabled
            • PCH TH Mem Buffer Size 1  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x716 (QID 0x39, 8b)
                help: Select size of memory region 1 buffer. Choose None/OS if OS-supported memory or trace forwarding is desired
                help: Note : Limit of total buffer size (PCH + CPU) is 512MB when iTBT disabled, 256MB when iTBT enabled
            • CPU Trace Hub Enable Mode  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x83 (QID 0x3A, 8b)
                help: Select 'Host Debugger' if Trace Hub is used with host debugger tool or 'Target Debugger' if Trace Hub is used
                help: by target debugger software. Note: If 'Host Debugger' is selected, Platform Debug Consent has to be ENABLED
                help: because DCI is one of the primary trace data output paths.
            • CPU TH Mem Buffer Size 0  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x84 (QID 0x3B, 8b)
                help: Select size of memory region 0 buffer. Choose None/OS if OS-supported memory or trace forwarding is desired
                help: Note : Limit of total buffer size (PCH + CPU) is 512MB when iTBT disabled, 256MB when iTBT enabled
            • CPU TH Mem Buffer Size 1  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x85 (QID 0x3C, 8b)
                help: Select size of memory region 1 buffer. Choose None/OS if OS-supported memory or trace forwarding is desired
                help: Note : Limit of total buffer size (PCH + CPU) is 512MB when iTBT disabled, 256MB when iTBT enabled
            • CPU Run Control   [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x111 (QID 0x3D, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable CPU Run Control Support; No Change : Comply with HW value
            • CPU Run Control Lock   [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x112 (QID 0x3E, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable CPU Run Control Lock
            • USB Overcurrent Override for VISA  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0xB (QID 0x3F, 8b)
                help: This option overrides USB Over Current enablement state that USB OC will be disabled after enabling this
                help: option. Enable when VISA pin is muxed with USB OC
            • Processor trace memory allocation  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x122 (QID 0x40, 8b)
                help: Disable or Select Processor trace memory region Size: from 4KB ~ 128MB
            • Processor trace  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x123 (QID 0x41, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable processor trace feature from CPU MSR. Enabling this feature will immediately start trace
                help: collection.
            • Processor Trace OutPut Scheme  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x121 (QID 0x42, 8b)
                help: Select Single Range Output scheme or ToPA table Output scheme
            • JTAG C10 Power Gate  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x8 (QID 0x43, 8b)
                help: When Enabled, JTAG is power gated in C10 state. When Disabled, keeps the JTAG power up during C10 and deeper
                help: power states for debug purpose.
            • Three Strike Counter  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x125 (QID 0x44, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Three Strike Counter
            • CrashLog Feature  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x836 (QID 0x45, 8b)
                help: The feature helps collecting crash data from PMC SSRAM
            • CrashLog On All Reset  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x837 (QID 0x46, 8b)
                help: Option to invoke CrashLog collection on all reset
            • CrashLog PMC Rearm  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0xB99 (QID 0x47, 8b)
                help: Option to invoke crashlog re-arm
            • CrashLog PMC Clear  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0xB98 (QID 0x48, 8b)
                help: Option to invoke CrashLog clear
            • CrashLog Cdie Rearm  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0xB97 (QID 0x49, 8b)
                help: Option to invoke crashlog re-arm
            • CrashLog Cdie Clear  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0xB96 (QID 0x4A, 8b)
                help: Option to invoke CrashLog clear
            • CrashLog GPRs  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x314 (QID 0x4B, 8b)
                help: Helps collecting crash data from PMC SSRAM. Enabling this may expose personal or confidential information
                help: that may be held in the GPRs at the time of the Crash trigger
            • PMC Debug Message Enable  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x741 (QID 0x4C, 8b)
                help: When Enabled, PMC HW will send debug messages to trace hub; When Disabled, PMC HW will never send debug
                help: meesages to trace hub. Noted: When Enabled, may not enter S0ix
            • Delayed Authentication Mode  [oneof]
                meta: MeSetupStorage @ 0x9 (QID 0x13CF, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Delayed Authentication Mode
    PCI Subsystem Settings  (0x1008)
        ▸ PCI Settings
        ▸ 
        • PCI ROM Priority  [oneof]
            meta: Setup @ 0xB (QID 0x4D, 8b)
            help: In case of multiple Option ROMs (Legacy and EFI Compatible), specifies what PCI Option rom to launch.
        • External DMA Allowed On Boot  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x1 (QID 0x4E, 8b)
            help: External DMA Allowed On Boot for devices such as 1394, PCMCIA, & CardBus
        • Install Ext OpRom Before BIOS Setup  [oneof]
            meta: PciBusSetup @ 0x0 (QID 0x4F, 8b)
            help: Install Ext OpRom for Storage or Other device before BIOS Setup, That we can get the Ext PCIE Card OpRom
            help: Setup Menu and have chance to enter into it
        • PCI Latency Timer  [oneof]
            meta: Setup @ 0xC (QID 0x50, 8b)
            help: Value to be programmed into PCI Latency Timer Register.
    ACPI Table/Features Control  (0x28)
        ▸ ACPI Table/Features Control
        ▸  
        • FACP - RTC S4 Wakeup  [oneof]
            meta: SystemConfig @ 0x53 (QID 0x2A, 8b)
            help: Value only for ACPI. Enable/Disable for S4 Wakeup from RTC
        • APIC - IO APIC Mode  [oneof]
            meta: SystemConfig @ 0x54 (QID 0x2B, 8b)
            help: This item is valid only for WIN2k and WINXP.Also,a fresh install of the OS must occur when APIC Mode is
            help: desired.Test the IO ACPI by setting item to Enable.The APIC Table will then be pointed to by the RSDT,the
            help: Local APIC will be initialized,and the proper enable bits will be set in ICH4M
        ACPI Settings  (0x100A)
            ▸ ACPI Settings
            ▸ 
            ▸ 
            • PECI Access Method  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x1B (QID 0x54, 8b)
                help: PECI Access Method is Direct I/O or ACPI
            • ACPI S3 Support  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0xE (QID 0x55, 8b)
                help: Enable ACPI S3 support
            • Native PCIE Enable  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x11 (QID 0x56, 8b)
                help: Bit - PCIe Native * control  0 - ~ Hot Plug  1 - SHPC Native Hot Plug control  2 - ~ Power Management Events 
                help: 3 - PCIe Advanced Error Reporting control  4 - PCIe Capability Structure control  5 - Latency Tolerance
                help: Reporting control
            • Native ASPM  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x12 (QID 0x57, 8b)
                help: Enabled - OS Controlled ASPM, Disabled - BIOS Controlled ASPM
            • BDAT ACPI Table Support  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x1 (QID 0x58, 8b)
                help: Enables support for the BDAT ACPI table.
            ▸ 
            • ACPI Debug  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x14 (QID 0x59, 8b)
                help: Open a memory buffer for storing debug strings. Reenter SETUP after enabling to see the buffer address. Use
                help: method ADBG to write strings to buffer.
            •   Print to Serial Port  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x83F (QID 0x5A, 8b)
                help: Print copy of ACPI debug logs to serial port
            • D3 Setting for Storage  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0xAB3 (QID 0x5B, 8b)
                help: RTD3 support for Storage. PCIE storage PEP constraint needs to be set as D0/F1 (Intel Advanced -> ACPI
                help: Settings -> PEP Constraints Configuration -> PEP PCIe Storage) when this setup is disabled/D3Hot
            • Low Power S0 Idle Capability  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x1C (QID 0x111D, 8b)
                help: This variable determines if we enable ACPI Lower Power S0 Idle Capability (Mutually exclusive with Smart
                help: connect). While this is enabled, it also disable 8254 timer for SLP_S0 support.
            • PUIS Enable  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x84F (QID 0x5C, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Power-Up In Standby (PUIS) feature set allows devices to be powered-up into the Standby power
                help: management state to minimize inrush current at power-up and to allow the host to sequence the spin-up of
                help: devices.
            • EC Notification  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x31 (QID 0x5D, 8b)
                help: Sends EC notification of Low Power S0 Idle State
            • EC CS Debug Light  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x32 (QID 0x1395, 8b)
                help: When EC enters Low Power S0 Idle State, the CAPS LOCK light will be turned on
            • EC Low Power Mode  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x33 (QID 0x5E, 8b)
                help: This option controls whether EC will go to Low power mode during Low Power S0 Idle State
            • Sensor Standby  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x38 (QID 0x5F, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Sensor standby mode
            • CS PL1 Limit  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x39 (QID 0x60, 8b)
                help: Limit PL1 (Power Limit 1) while in Connected Standby
            • CS PL1 Value  [numeric]
                meta: Setup @ 0x3A (QID 0x61, 16b)
                help: PL1 value is in milliwatts with 125 step value
            • LPIT Residency Counter  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x3E (QID 0x63, 8b)
                help: Select Residency Counter
            ▸ 
            • SSDT table from file  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x40 (QID 0x64, 8b)
                help: SSDT table from file
            ▸ 
            • PCI Delay Optimization  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x19 (QID 0x65, 8b)
                help: Experimental ACPI additions for FW latency optimizations
            • MSI enabled  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x786 (QID 0x66, 8b)
                help: When disabled, MSI support is disabled in FADT

    CPU Configuration  (0x100D)
        ▸ CPU Configuration
        ▸ 
        ▸ 
        ▸ 
        • C6DRAM  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x114 (QID 0x8001, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable moving of DRAM contents to PRM memory when CPU is in C6 state
        • CPU Flex Ratio Override  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x3 (QID 0x88, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable CPU Flex Ratio Programming
        • CPU Flex Ratio Settings  [numeric]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1 (QID 0x10E1, 8b)
            help: This value must be between Max Efficiency Ratio (LFM) and Maximum non-turbo ratio set by Hardware (HFM).
        • Hardware Prefetcher  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xBF (QID 0x89, 8b)
            help: To turn on/off the MLC streamer prefetcher.
        • Adjacent Cache Line Prefetch  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xC0 (QID 0x8A, 8b)
            help: To turn on/off prefetching of adjacent cache lines.
        • Intel (VMX) Virtualization Technology  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xB9 (QID 0x1419, 8b)
            help: When enabled, a VMM can utilize the additional hardware capabilities provided by Vanderpool Technology.
        • PECI  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x4 (QID 0x8B, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable PECI
        • AVX  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2DC (QID 0x8C, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable the Avx 2 Instructions. This is applicable for Performance-core only
        • Active Performance-cores  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x6 (QID 0x8D, 8b)
            help: Number of P-cores to enable in each processor package. Note: Number of Cores and E-cores are looked at
            help: together. When both are {0,0}, Pcode will enable all cores.
        • Active Efficient-cores  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x22F (QID 0x8E, 8b)
            help: Number of E-cores to enable in each processor package. Note: Number of Cores and E-cores are looked at
            help: together. When both are {0,0}, Pcode will enable all cores.
        • Hyper-Threading  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x5 (QID 0x1417, 8b)
            help: Enable or Disable Hyper-Threading Technology.
        • BIST  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x7 (QID 0x8F, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable BIST (Built-In Self Test) on reset
        • AP threads Idle Manner  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x120 (QID 0x90, 8b)
            help: AP threads Idle Manner for waiting signal to run
        • AES  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xBA (QID 0x91, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable AES (Advanced Encryption Standard)
        • MachineCheck  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xBB (QID 0x92, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable Machine Check
        • MonitorMWait  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xBC (QID 0x93, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable MonitorMWait, if Disable MonitorMwait, the AP threads Idle Manner should not set in MWAIT Loop
        • Intel Trusted Execution Technology  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xC1 (QID 0x1549, 8b)
            help: Enables utilization of additional hardware capabilities provided by Intel (R) Trusted Execution Technology. 
            help: Changes require a full power cycle to take effect.
        • Alias Check Request  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xC4 (QID 0x94, 8b)
            help: Enables Txt Alias Checking capability  Changes require full Txt capability before it will take effect.   It
            help: is a one time only change, next reboot will be reset.
        • DPR Memory Size (MB)  [numeric]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xBD (QID 0x95, 16b)
            help: Reserve DPR memory size (0-255) MB
        • Reset AUX Content  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0xC3 (QID 0x96, 8b)
            help: Reset TPM Aux content. Txt may not functional after AUX content gets reset.
        • FCLK Frequency for Early Power On  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x126 (QID 0x99, 8b)
            help: FCLK frequency can take values of 400MHz, 800MHz  and 1GHz (1GHz not supported for ULT/ULX SKUs)
        • Total Memory Encryption  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x229 (QID 0x8002, 8b)
            help: Configure Total Memory Encryption (TME) to protect DRAM data from physical attacks.
        • Legacy Game Compatibility Mode  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x34D (QID 0x1405, 8b)
            help: When enabled, Pressing the scroll lock key will toggle the Efficient-cores between being parked when Scroll
            help: Lock LED is on and un-parked when LED is off.

    Connectivity Configuration  (0x13D2)
        •   CNVi Mode  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x734 (QID 0x18B, 8b)
            help: This option configures Connectivity.  [Auto Detection] means that if Discrete solution is discovered it will
            help: be enabled by default. Otherwise Integrated solution (CNVi) will be enabled;  [Disable Integrated] disables
            help: Integrated Solution.  NOTE: When CNVi is present, the GPIO pins that are used for radio interface cannot be
            help: assigned to the other native function.
        •   Wi-Fi Core  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x735 (QID 0x18C, 8b)
            help: This is an option intended to Enable/Disable Wi-Fi Core in CNVi
        •   BT Core  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x736 (QID 0x18D, 8b)
            help: This is an option intended to Enable/Disable BT Core in CNVi
        •   BT RF-Kill Delay Time  [numeric]
            meta: Setup @ 0xB94 (QID 0x18E, 16b)
            help: This is the delay Time to switch on/off BT RF-KILL signal during S3/S4 resume. Unit is ms
        • BT Audio Offload  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x737 (QID 0x146E, 8b)
            help: This is an option to Enable/Disable BT Audio Offload which enables audio input from BT device to the audio
            help: DSP and enables power efficient audio output to BT device.
        • RFI Mitigation  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x738 (QID 0x18F, 8b)
            help: This is an option intended to Enable/Disable DDR-RFIM feature for Connectivity This RFI mitigation feature
            help: may result in temporary slowdown of the DDR speed.
        • DLVR RFI Mitigation  [oneof]
            meta: Setup @ 0xBA8 (QID 0x190, 8b)
            help: This is an option intended to Enable/Disable DLVR RFIM feature for Connectivity This option is only valid
            help: when global DLVR is enabled.
        ▸ 
        • CoExistence Manager  [oneof]
            meta: Setup @ 0x772 (QID 0x191, 8b)
            help: CoEx Manager mitigates radio coexistence issues between Intel WWAN (modem) and Intel WLAN (WiFi/BT). This
            help: should be enabled only if both WWAN and WLAN solutions are based on Intel components
        ▸

            meta: Setup @ 0x773 (QID 0x192, 8b)
            help: This will be used to enable Preboot Bluetooth function

            help: Defines the WiFi SAR Sets that can be used to set the power limts dynamically based on the Proximity
            help: Sensor,Set 1 is always present if WiFi SAR enabled and Set 2-3 are additinoal sets

                help: Enable or Disable WCCD ACPI device node
            • WWAN Reset Workaround  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x84E (QID 0x2EE, 8b)
                help: Enabling this workardound will result in BIOS asserting FULL_CARD_POWER_OFF#, PERST# and RESET# WWAN signals
                help: before the WWAN Device Power-On Sequence is executed. Disabling it has no impact.

                help: WWAN OEM Sub-Vendor ID

        CPU - Power Management Control  (0x100F)
            ▸ CPU - Power Management Control
            ▸ 
            • Boot performance mode  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x13 (QID 0x1578, 8b)
                help: Select the performance state that the BIOS will set starting from reset vector.
            • Intel(R) SpeedStep(tm)  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x9 (QID 0x140A, 8b)
                help: Allows more than two frequency ranges to be supported.
            • Race To Halt (RTH)  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xA (QID 0xA2, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Race To Halt feature. RTH will dynamically increase CPU frequency in order to enter pkg
                help: C-State faster to reduce overall power. (RTH is controlled through MSR 1FC bit 20)
            • Intel(R) Speed Shift Technology  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xB (QID 0x1408, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Intel(R) Speed Shift Technology support. Enabling will expose the CPPC v2 interface to allow
                help: for hardware controlled P-states.
            • Intel(R) Turbo Boost Max Technology 3.0  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xC (QID 0x1409, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Intel(R) Turbo Boost Max Technology 3.0 support. Disabling will report the maximum ratio of
                help: the slowest core in _CPC object.
            • Per Core P State OS control mode  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xE (QID 0xA3, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Per Core P state OS control mode. Disabling will set Bit 31 = 1 command 0x06. When set, the
                help: highest core request is used for all other core requests.
            • HwP Autonomous Per Core P State  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xF (QID 0xA4, 8b)
                help: Disable Autonomous PCPS (Bit 30 = 1, command 0x11) Autonomous will request the same value for all cores all
                help: the time. Enable PCPS (default Bit 30 = 0, command 0x11)
            • HwP Autonomous EPP Grouping  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x10 (QID 0xA5, 8b)
                help: Enable EPP grouping (default Bit 29 =0, command 0x11) Autonomous will request the same values for all cores
                help: with same EPP. Disable EPP grouping (Bit 29 =1 , command 0x11) autonomous will not necessarily request same
                help: values for all cores with same EPP.
            • EPB override over PECI  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x11 (QID 0xA6, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable EPB override over PECI. Enable by sending pcode command 0x2b , subcommand 0x3 to 1. This will
                help: allow OOB EPB PECI override control
            • HwP Fast MSR Support  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x12 (QID 0xA7, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable HwP Fast MSR Support for IA32_HWP_REQUEST MSR.
            • HwP Lock  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x228 (QID 0xA8, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable HWP Lock support in Misc Power Management MSR.
            • HDC Control  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x4A (QID 0xA9, 8b)
                help: This option allows HDC configuration.  Disabled: Disable HDC  Enabled:  Can be enabled by OS if OS native
                help: support is available.
            •   Turbo Mode  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x16 (QID 0x1579, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable processor Turbo Mode (requires EMTTM enabled too). AUTO means enabled.
            • Platform PL1 Enable  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x31 (QID 0xAD, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Platform Power Limit 1 programming. If this option is enabled, it activates the PL1 value to
                help: be used by the processor to limit the average power of given time window.
            • Platform PL1 Power  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x32 (QID 0xAE, 32b)
                help: Platform Power Limit 1 Power in Milli Watts/Percent. BIOS will round to the nearest 1/8W when programming.
                help: Any value can be programmed between Max and Min Power Limits (specified by PACKAGE_POWER_SKU_MSR). For
                help: example, if 12.50W, enter 12500, if 12%, enter 12000, if 50%, enter 50000. This setting will act as the new
                help: PL1 value for the Package RAPL algorithm.
            • Platform PL1 Time Window  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x36 (QID 0xAF, 8b)
                help: Platform Power Limit 1 Time Window value in seconds. The value may vary from 0 to 128. 0 = default values.
                help: Indicates the time window over which Platform Processor Base Power (TDP) value should be maintained.
            • Platform PL2 Enable  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x37 (QID 0xB0, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Platform Power Limit 2 programming. If this option is disabled, BIOS will program the default
                help: values for Platform Power Limit 2.
            • Platform PL2 Power  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x38 (QID 0xB1, 32b)
                help: Platform Power Limit 2 Power in Milli Watts / Milli Percent. BIOS will round to the nearest 1/8W or 1/8% when
                help: programming. Any value can be programmed between Max and Min Power Limits (specified by
                help: PACKAGE_POWER_SKU_MSR) or PMAX value in %. For example if 12%, enter 12000, if 50%, enter 50000. If the value
                help: is '0', will default to PACKAGE_POWER_SKU_MSR if Psys is in Watts. Recommended value is 97000 if Psys is in
                help: %. This setting will act as the new PL2 value for the Package RAPL algorithm.
            • ATX Telemetry Unit  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x384 (QID 0xB2, 8b)
                help: ATX Telemetry Unit in Watts or Percentage.
            • Power Limit 4 Override  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2A (QID 0xB3, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Power Limit 4 override. If this option is disabled, BIOS will leave the default values for
                help: Power Limit 4.
            • Power Limit 4  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2B (QID 0xB4, 32b)
                help: Power Limit 4 in Milli Watts. BIOS will round to the nearest 1/8W when programming. For 12.50W, enter 12500.
                help: If the value is 0, BIOS leaves default value
            • Power Limit 4 Lock  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2F (QID 0xB5, 8b)
                help: Power Limit 4 MSR 601h Lock. When enabled PL4 configurations are locked during OS. When disabled PL4
                help: configuration can be changed during OS.
            •   Enhanced C-states  [oneof]

            • IO MWAIT Redirection  [oneof]

                help: Interrupt Redirection Mode Select for Logical Interrupts
            • Timed MWAIT   [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x47 (QID 0xCA, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Timed MWAIT Support
            • EC Turbo Control Mode  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xC7 (QID 0xCC, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable EC Turbo Control mode
            •   AC Brick Capacity  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xC8 (QID 0xCD, 8b)
                help: Specify the AC Brick capacity
            •   EC Polling Period  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xC9 (QID 0xCE, 8b)
                help: Count 1 to 255 for a range of 10ms to 2.55 seconds (1 count = 10ms)
            •   EC Guard Band Value  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xCA (QID 0xCF, 8b)
                help: Count 1 to 20 for a range of 1 watt to 20 watts
            •   EC Algorithm Selection  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0xCB (QID 0xD0, 8b)
                help: Count 1 to 10 for Algorithm Selection
            • Energy Performance Gain  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x75 (QID 0xD1, 8b)
                help: Enable/disable Energy Performance Gain.
            • EPG DIMM Idd3N  [numeric]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x76 (QID 0xD2, 16b)
                help: Active standby current (Idd3N) in milliamps from datasheet. Must be calculated on a per DIMM basis.
            • EPG DIMM Idd3P  [numeric]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x78 (QID 0xD3, 16b)
                help: Active power-down current (Idd3P) in milliamps from datasheet. Must be calculated on a per DIMM basis.
            • Dual Tau Boost  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2AD (QID 0xD6, 8b)
                help: Enable Dual Tau Boost feature. This is only applicable for Desktop 35W/65W/125W sku. When DPTF is enabled
                help: this feature is ignored.
              View/Configure Turbo Options  (0x1011)
                ▸ Current Turbo Settings
                ▸ 
                ▸ 
                • Energy Efficient P-state  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x3C (QID 0x111, 8b)
                    help: Enable/Disable Energy Efficient P-state feature. When set to 0, will disable access to
                    help: ENERGY_PERFORMANCE_BIAS MSR and CPUID Function 6 ECX[3] will read 0 indicating no support for Energy
                    help: Efficient policy setting. When set to 1 will enable access to ENERGY_PERFORMANCE_BIAS MSR 1B0h and CPUID
                    help: Function 6 ECX[3] will read 1 indicating Energy Efficient policy setting is supported.
                • Package Power Limit MSR Lock  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x30 (QID 0x112, 8b)
                    help: Enable/Disable locking of Package Power Limit settings. When enabled, PACKAGE_POWER_LIMIT MSR will be locked
                    help: and a reset will be required to unlock the register.
                • Power Limit 1 Override  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1B (QID 0x113, 8b)
                    help: Enable/Disable Power Limit 1 override. If this option is disabled, BIOS will program the default values for
                    help: Power Limit 1 and Power Limit 1 Time Window.
                • Power Limit 1  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x17 (QID 0x114, 32b)
                    help: Power Limit 1 in Milli Watts. BIOS will round to the nearest 1/8W when programming. 0 = no custom override.
                    help: For 12.50W, enter 12500. Overclocking SKU: Value must be between Max and Min Power Limits (specified by
                    help: PACKAGE_POWER_SKU_MSR). Other SKUs: This value must be between Min Power Limit and Processor Base Power (TDP)
                    help: Limit. If value is 0, BIOS will program Processor Base Power (TDP) value.
                • Power Limit 1 Time Window  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1C (QID 0x115, 8b)
                    help: Power Limit 1 Time Window value in seconds. The value may vary from 0 to 128. 0 = default value. Defines time
                    help: window which Processor Base Power (TDP) value should be maintained.
                • Power Limit 2 Override  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1D (QID 0x116, 8b)
                    help: Enable/Disable Power Limit 2 override. If this option is disabled, BIOS will program the default values for
                    help: Power Limit 2.
                • Power Limit 2  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1E (QID 0x117, 32b)
                    help: Power Limit 2 value in Milli Watts. BIOS will round to the nearest 1/8W when programming. If the value is 0,
                    help: BIOS will program this value as 1.25*Processor Base Power (TDP). For 12.50W, enter 12500. Processor applies
                    help: control policies such that the package power does not exceed this limit.
                • Energy Efficient Turbo  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1D5 (QID 0x118, 8b)
                    help: Enable/Disable Energy Efficient Turbo Feature. This feature will opportunistically lower the turbo frequency
                    help: to increase efficiency. Recommended only to disable in overclocking situations where turbo frequency must
                    help: remain constant. Otherwise, leave enabled.

              Config TDP Configurations  (0x10E9)
                ▸ Config TDP Configurations
                ▸ 
                • Enable Configurable TDP  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x227 (QID 0x179, 8b)
                    help: Applies Configurable Processor Base Power (cTDP) initialization settings based on non-cTDP or cTDP. Default
                    help: is 1: Applies to cTDP; if 0 then applies non-cTDP and BIOS will bypass cTDP initialzation flow
                • Configurable TDP Boot Mode  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x44 (QID 0x17A, 8b)
                    help: Configurable Processor Base Power (cTDP) Mode as Nominal/Level/Leve2/Deactivate TDP selection. Deactivate
                    help: option will set MSR to Nominal and MMIO to Zero.
                • Configurable TDP Lock  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x45 (QID 0x17B, 8b)
                    help: Configurable Processor Base Power (cTDP) Mode Lock sets the Lock bits on TURBO_ACTIVATION_RATIO and
                    help: CONFIG_TDP_CONTROL.                 Note: When CTDP Lock is enabled Custom ConfigTDP Count will be forced to
                    help: 1 and Custom ConfigTDP Boot Index will be forced to 0.

            CPU VR Settings  (0x13B7)
                ▸ CPU VR Settings
                ▸ 
                • PSYS Slope  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x127 (QID 0x119, 8b)
                    help: PSYS Slope defined in 1/100 increments. Range is 0-200. For a 1.25 slope, enter 125. 0 = AUTO. Uses BIOS VR
                    help: mailbox command 0x9.
                • PSYS Offset  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x128 (QID 0x11A, 16b)
                    help: PSYS Offset defined in 1/1000 increments. Range is 0-63999. For an offset of 25.348, enter 25348. PSYS Uses
                    help: BIOS VR mailbox command 0x4.
                •   PSYS Prefix  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x12A (QID 0x11B, 8b)
                    help: Sets the offset value as positive or negative.
                • PSYS PMax Power  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x12B (QID 0x11C, 16b)
                    help: Psys Pmax power, defined in 1/8 Watt or 1/8 Percent increments. For Watts, range is 0-8191 (ex. For 125W,
                    help: enter 1000). For ATX12VO Percent, Range is 0-1600 (ex. For recommended value of 200%, enter 1600). Uses BIOS
                    help: VR mailbox command 0xB.
                • Min Voltage Override  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x207 (QID 0x11D, 8b)
                    help: Min Voltage Override. Enable to override minimum voltage for runtime and for C8.
                •   Min Voltage Runtime  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x208 (QID 0x11E, 16b)
                    help: Min Voltage for Runtime. Range is 0 - 1999mV in 1/128 volt increments. Input is in mVolts.
                •   Min Voltage C8  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x20A (QID 0x11F, 16b)
                    help: Min Voltage for Package C8. Range is 0 - 1999mV in 1/128 volt increments. Input is in mVolts.
                • VccIn Aux Icc Max  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2E8 (QID 0x120, 16b)
                    help: Sets the Max Icc VccIn Aux value defined in 1/4A increments. Range is 0-512. For an IccMax 32A, enter
                    help: 128(32*4).
                • VccIn Aux IMON Slope  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2EA (QID 0x121, 16b)
                    help: VccIN Aux IMON Slope defined in 1/100 increments. Range is 0-200. For a 1.25 slope, enter 125. 0 = AUTO. Uses
                    help: BIOS VR mailbox command 0x18.
                • VccIN Aux IMON Offset  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2EC (QID 0x122, 16b)
                    help: VccIN Aux IMON Offset defined in 1/1000 increments. Range is 0-63999. For an offset of 25.348, enter 25348.
                    help: IMON Uses BIOS VR mailbox command 0x18.
                •   VccIN Aux IMON Prefix  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2EE (QID 0x123, 8b)
                    help: Sets the offset value as positive or negative.
                • Vsys/Psys Critical  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2EF (QID 0x124, 8b)
                    help: Vsys/Psys Critical Enable or disable
                • Vsys/Psys Full Scale  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x38D (QID 0x125, 32b)
                    help: Input Vsys or Psys Full-Scale and Critical Threshold to program Vsys/Psys Critical (0x4A) register. Critical
                    help: Limit Trigger = (Critical Threshold/Full-Scale) * 0xFF. Input for Vsys is in Milli-Volts. For Psys either in
                    help: Milli-Watts or Milli-Percent. For ATX12VO systems, recommended value is 200000.
                • Vsys/Psys Critical Threshold  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x391 (QID 0x126, 32b)
                    help: Input Vsys or Psys Full-Scale and Critical Threshold to program Vsys/Psys Critical (0x4A) register. Critical
                    help: Limit Trigger = (Critical Threshold/Full-Scale) * 0xFF. Input for Vsys is in Milli-Volts. For Psys either in
                    help: Milli-Watts or Milli-Percent. For ATX12VO systems, recommended value is 120000.
                • Vsys/Psys Full Scale  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x385 (QID 0x127, 32b)
                    help: Input Vsys or Psys Full-Scale and Critical Threshold to program Vsys/Psys Critical (0x4A) register. Critical
                    help: Limit Trigger = (Critical Threshold/Full-Scale) * 0xFF. Input for Vsys is in Milli-Volts. For Psys either in
                    help: Milli-Watts or Milli-Percent. For ATX12VO systems, recommended value is 200000.
                • Vsys/Psys Critical Threshold  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x389 (QID 0x128, 32b)
                    help: Input Vsys or Psys Full-Scale and Critical Threshold to program Vsys/Psys Critical (0x4A) register. Critical
                    help: Limit Trigger = (Critical Threshold/Full-Scale) * 0xFF. Input for Vsys is in Milli-Volts. For Psys either in
                    help: Milli-Watts or Milli-Percent. For ATX12VO systems, recommended value is 120000.
                • Assertion Deglitch Mantissa   [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2F2 (QID 0x129, 8b)
                    help: Assertion Deglitch Mantissa 0x4F[7-3]. Assertion Deglitch = 2µs * Mantissa * 2^(Exponent)
                • Assertion Deglitch Exponent   [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2F3 (QID 0x12A, 8b)
                    help: Assertion Deglitch Exponent 0x4F[3-0]. Assertion Deglitch = 2µs * Mantissa * 2^(Exponent)
                • De assertion Deglitch Mantissa  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2F4 (QID 0x12B, 8b)
                    help: De Assertion Deglitch Mantissa 0x49[7-3]. Assertion Deglitch = 2µs * Mantissa * 2^(Exponent)
                • De assertion Deglitch Exponent  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2F5 (QID 0x12C, 8b)
                    help: De Assertion Deglitch Exponent 0x49[3-0]. Assertion Deglitch = 2µs * Mantissa * 2^(Exponent)
                • VR Power Delivery Design  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x313 (QID 0x10C2, 8b)
                    help: Specifies the ADL Desktop board design used for the VR settings override values. By default, BIOS will
                    help: override the default Desktop VR settings based on the board design. A value of AUTO(0) will use the board ID
                    help: to determine the board design. Any other value will override the board id logic to provide a custom VR Power
                    help: Delivery Design value. This is intended primarily for validation.
                Acoustic Noise Settings  (0x13BA)
                    ▸ Acoustic Noise Settings
                    ▸ 
                    • Acoustic Noise Mitigation  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1FC (QID 0x132, 8b)
                        help: Enabling this option will help mitigate acoustic noise on certain SKUs when the CPU is in deeper C state
                    • Pre Wake Time  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2D9 (QID 0x133, 8b)
                        help: Set the maximum Pre Wake randomization time in micro ticks. Range is 0-255. This is for acoustic noise
                        help: mitigation Dynamic Perodicity Alteration (DPA) tuning.
                    • Ramp Up Time  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2DA (QID 0x134, 8b)
                        help: Set the maximum Ramp Up randomization time in micro ticks. Range is 0-255. This is for acoustic noise
                        help: mitigation Dynamic Perodicity Alteration (DPA) tuning.
                    • Ramp Down Time  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2DB (QID 0x135, 8b)
                        help: Set the maximum Ramp Down randomization time in micro ticks. Range is 0-255. This is for acoustic noise
                        help: mitigation Dynamic Perodicity Alteration (DPA) tuning.
                    ▸ 
                    ▸ IA VR Domain
                    • Disable Fast PKG C State Ramp for IA Domain  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1FD (QID 0x136, 8b)
                        help: This option needs to be configured to reduce acoustic noise during deeper C states. False: Don't disable Fast
                        help: ramp during deeper C states; True: Disable Fast ramp during deeper C state
                    • Slow Slew Rate for IA Domain  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x202 (QID 0x137, 8b)
                        help: Set VR IA Slow Slew Rate for Deep Package C State ramp time; Slow slew rate equals to Fast divided by number,
                        help: the number is 2, 4, 8, 16 to slow down the slew rate to help minimize acoustic noise
                    ▸ 
                    ▸ GT VR Domain
                    • Disable Fast PKG C State Ramp for GT Domain  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1FE (QID 0x138, 8b)
                        help: This option needs to be configured to reduce acoustic noise during deeper C states. False: Don't disable Fast
                        help: ramp during deeper C states; True: Disable Fast ramp during deeper C state
                    • Slow Slew Rate for GT Domain  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x203 (QID 0x139, 8b)
                        help: Set VR GT Slow Slew Rate for Deep Package C State ramp time; Slow slew rate equals to Fast divided by number,
                        help: the number is 2, 4, 8 to slow down the slew rate to help minimize acoustic noise; divide by 16 is disabled
                    ▸ 
                    ▸ SA VR Domain
                    • Disable Fast PKG C State Ramp for SA Domain  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1FF (QID 0x13A, 8b)
                        help: This option needs to be configured to reduce acoustic noise during deeper C states. False: Don't disable Fast
                        help: ramp during deeper C states; True: Disable Fast ramp during deeper C state
                Core/IA VR Settings  (0x13B9)
                    ▸ Core/IA VR Domain
                    ▸ 
                    • VR Config Enable  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x12D (QID 0x13B, 8b)
                        help: VR Config Enable
                    • AC Loadline  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x132 (QID 0x13C, 16b)
                        help: AC Loadline defined in 1/100 mOhms. A value of 100 = 1.00 mOhm, and 1255 = 12.55 mOhm. Range is 0-6249
                        help: (0-62.49 mOhms). 0 = AUTO/HW default. Uses BIOS mailbox command 0x2.
                    • DC Loadline  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x13C (QID 0x13D, 16b)
                        help: DC Loadline defined in 1/100 mOhms. A value of 100 = 1.00 mOhm, and 1255 = 12.55 mOhm. Range is 0-6249
                        help: (0-62.49 mOhms). 0 = AUTO/HW default. Uses BIOS mailbox command 0x2.
                    • PS Current Threshold1  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x146 (QID 0x13E, 16b)
                        help: PS Current Threshold1, defined in 1/4 A increments. A value of 400 = 100A. Range 0-512, which translates to
                        help: 0-128A. 0 = AUTO. Uses BIOS VR mailbox command 0x3.
                    • PS Current Threshold2  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x150 (QID 0x13F, 16b)
                        help: PS Current Threshold2, defined in 1/4 A increments. A value of 400 = 100A. Range 0-512, which translates to
                        help: 0-128A. 0 = AUTO. Uses BIOS VR mailbox command 0x3.
                    • PS Current Threshold3  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x15A (QID 0x140, 16b)
                        help: PS Current Threshold3, defined in 1/4 A increments. A value of 400 = 100A. Range 0-512, which translates to
                        help: 0-128A. 0 = AUTO. Uses BIOS VR mailbox command 0x3.
                    • PS3 Enable  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x164 (QID 0x141, 8b)
                        help: PS3 Enable/Disable. 0 - Disabled, 1 - Enabled.Uses BIOS VR mailbox command 0x3.
                    • PS4 Enable  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x169 (QID 0x142, 8b)
                        help: PS4 Enable/Disable. 0 - Disabled, 1 - Enabled. Uses BIOS VR mailbox command 0x3
                    • IMON Slope  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x16E (QID 0x143, 16b)
                        help: IMON Slope defined in 1/100 increments. Range is 0-200. For a 1.25 slope, enter 125. 0 = AUTO. Uses BIOS VR
                        help: mailbox command 0x4.
                    • IMON Offset  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x178 (QID 0x144, 16b)
                        help: IMON Offset defined in 1/1000 increments. Range is 0-63999. For an offset of 25.348, enter 25348. IMON Uses
                        help: BIOS VR mailbox command 0x4.
                    •   IMON Prefix  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x182 (QID 0x145, 8b)
                        help: Sets the offset value as positive or negative.
                    • VR Current Limit  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x187 (QID 0x146, 16b)
                        help: Voltage Regulator Current Limit (IccMax). This value represents the Maximum instantaneous current allowed at
                        help: any given time. The value is represented in 1/4 A increments. A value of 400 = 100A. 0 means AUTO. Uses BIOS
                        help: VR mailbox command 0x6.
                    • Core VR Fast Vmode  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x379 (QID 0x10C3, 8b)
                        help: Core VR Fast Vmode. Use to control Core Fast Vmode Enable/Disable. The value will only be effective by
                        help: enabling the corresponding CEP..
                    •   Fast Vmode Itrip ICC Limit  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x32A (QID 0x10C6, 16b)
                        help: Voltage Regulator Fast Vmode Itrip ICC Limit. A value of 400 = 100A. A value of 0 corresponds to feature
                        help: disabled (no reactive protection). This value represents the current threshold where the VR would initiate
                        help: reactive protection if Fast Vmode is enabled. The value is represented in 1/4 A increments. Uses BIOS VR
                        help: mailbox command 0x25.
                    • Core CEP Enable  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x396 (QID 0x147, 8b)
                        help: Enable/Disable the IA Core CEP (Current Excursion Protection) Support. BIOS doesn't allow to enable FVM when
                        help: CEP disabled
                    • VR Voltage Limit  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1BE (QID 0x148, 16b)
                        help: Voltage Limit (VMAX). This value represents the Maximum instantaneous voltage allowed at any given time.
                        help: Range is 0 - 7999mV. Uses BIOS VR mailbox command 0x8.
                    • TDC Enable  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x19B (QID 0x149, 8b)
                        help: TDC Enable. 0- Disable, 1 - Enable
                    • TDC Current Limit  [numeric]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x191 (QID 0x14A, 16b)
                        help: TDC Current Limit, defined in 1/8A increments. Range 0-32767. For a TDC Current Limit of 125A, enter 1000. 0
                        help: = 0 Amps. Uses BIOS VR mailbox command 0x1A.
                    • TDC Time Window  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1A0 (QID 0x14B, 32b)
                        help: VR TDC Time Window, value in seconds. 1s is default. Range from 1s to 448s.
                    • TDC Lock  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1B4 (QID 0x14C, 8b)
                        help: TDC Lock
                    • IRMS  [oneof]
                        meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1B9 (QID 0x14D, 8b)
                        help: Enable/Disable IRMS - Current root mean square

            Power Limit 3 Settings  (0x1111)
                • Power Limit 3 Override   [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x22 (QID 0x173, 8b)
                    help: Enable/DisablePower Limit 3 override. If this option is disabled, BIOS will leave the hardware default values
                    help: for Power Limit 3 and Power Limit 3 Time Window.
                • Power Limit 3  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x23 (QID 0x174, 32b)
                    help: Platform Power Limit 3 Power in Milli Watts / Milli Percent. BIOS will round to the nearest 1/8W or 1/8% when
                    help: programming. For example if 12%, enter 12000, if 50%, enter 50000.  For all SKUs, limit must be less than
                    help: Psys Pmax value (either in Watts or %). XE SKU: Any value up through Psys Pmax can be programmed.
                    help: Overclocking SKU: value must be between Max and Min Power Limits (specified by PACKAGE_POWER_SKU_MSR). Other
                    help: SKUs: This value must be between Min Power Limit and Processor Base Power (TDP). If the value is '0', will
                    help: default to hardware default value. Recommended value is 100000 if Psys is in %.
                • Power Limit 3 Time Window  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x27 (QID 0x175, 8b)
                    help: Power Limit 3 Time Window value in Milli seconds. The value may vary from 3 to 64(max). Indicates the time
                    help: window over which Power Limit 3 value should be maintained. If the value is 0, BIOS leaves the hardware
                    help: default value. For ATX12VO PSU systems, recommended value is 40.
                • Power Limit 3 Duty Cycle  [numeric]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x28 (QID 0x176, 8b)
                    help: Specify the duty cycle in percentage that the CPU is required to maintain over the configured time window.
                    help: Range is 0-100. For ATX12VO PSU systems, recommended value is 25.
                • Response Mode  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x383 (QID 0x177, 8b)
                    help: Use Response Mode to adjust Psys_PL3 power reduction behavior. Battery-enabled systems use Gradual power
                    help: reduction.
                • Power Limit 3 Lock  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x29 (QID 0x178, 8b)
                    help: Power Limit 3 MSR 615h Lock. When enabled PL3 configurations are locked during OS. When disabled PL3
                    help: configuration can be changed during OS.
              View/Configure CPU Lock Options  (0x1012)
                • CFG Lock  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x43 (QID 0x189, 8b)
                    help: Configure MSR 0xE2[15], CFG Lock bit
                • Overclocking Lock  [oneof]
                    meta: CpuSetup @ 0x10E (QID 0x18A, 8b)
                    help: Enable/Disable Overclocking Lock (BIT 20) in FLEX_RATIO(194) MSR

    OverClocking Performance Menu  (0x1013)
        ▸ OverClocking Performance Menu
        ▸ 
        • OverClocking Feature  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1D9 (QID 0x1574, 8b)
            help: Performance Menu for Processor and Memory.
        • UnderVolt Protection  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x381 (QID 0x2F1, 8b)
            help: When UnderVolt Protection is enabled, user will not be able to program under voltage in OS runtime.
            help: Recommended to keep it enabled by default. Enabled: Allow BIOS undervolting, but enable UnderVolt Protection
            help: in Runtime. Disabled: No UnderVolt Protection in Runtime.
        • Per-core HT Disable  [numeric]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x230 (QID 0x2F2, 16b)
        • WDT Enable  [oneof]
            meta: PchSetup @ 0x24 (QID 0x2F3, 8b)
            help: Enable/Disable WatchDog Timer. Note: This option is ignored on debug BIOS.
        • CPU BCLK OC Frequency  [numeric]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2F7 (QID 0x2F4, 32b)
            help: CPU BCLK Frequency in 10kHz increments. Specifies new CPU BCLK frequency to be applied. Frequency is rounded
            help: to nearest valid value. Allowed range is limited by the Max/Min supported frequencies. Changes are not
            help: applied until settings are saved and platform resets. Example: For 125.70MHz, enter 12570.
        • BCLK Aware Adaptive Voltage  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x20D (QID 0x2F5, 8b)
            help: BCLK Aware Adaptive Voltage enable/disable. When enabled, pcode will be aware of the BCLK frequency when
            help: calculating the CPU V/F curves. This is ideal for BCLK OC to avoid high voltage overrides. Uses OC Mailbox
            help: command 0x15.
        • PVD Ratio Threshold  [numeric]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1DC (QID 0x2F6, 8b)
            help: Select PVD Ratio Threshold Value from Range 1 to 40. 0 - Auto/HW default
        • FLL Overclock Mode Enable  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x352 (QID 0x2F7, 8b)
            help: Enable FLL Overclock Mode, Default is Disable
        •   FLL Overclock Mode Select  [numeric]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x353 (QID 0x2F8, 8b)
            help: Select FLL Mode Value from Range 1 to 3. 0x0 = no overclocking, 0x1 = ratio overclocking with nominal
            help: (0.5-1x) reference clock frequency, 0x2 = BCLK overclocking with elevated (1-3x) reference clock frequency,
            help: 0x3 = BCLK overclocking with extreme elevated (3-5x) reference clock frequency and ratio limited to 63
        • SA PLL Frequency Override  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x342 (QID 0x2F9, 8b)
            help: Configure Sa PLL Frequency.
        • BCLK TSC HW Fixup  [oneof]
            meta: CpuSetup @ 0x344 (QID 0x2FA, 8b)
            help: BCLK TSC HW Fixup disable during TSC copy from PMA to APIC

            ▸ 
            • AVX2 Ratio Offset  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1E5 (QID 0x352, 8b)
                help: AVX2 Ratio Offset. Specifies number of bins to decrease AVX ratio vs. Core Ratio. AVX is a more stressful
                help: workload, it is helpful to lower the AVX ratio to ensure maximum possible ratio for SSE workloads. Uses
                help: Mailbox MSR 0x150, cmd 0x1B. Range 0 - 31. 0 = No offset.
            • AVX2 Voltage Guardband Scale Factor  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x225 (QID 0x353, 8b)
                help: AVX2 Voltage Guardband Scale Factor. Controls the voltage guardband applied to AVX workloads. Range 0 - 200
                help: in 1/100 units, where 125 = 1.25 scale factor. A default value of 100 applies the default voltage guardband
                help: scale factor of 1.0. A value > 100 will increase the votlage guardband, and < 100 will decrease the voltage
                help: guardband.
            ▸ 
            • TjMax Offset  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x1F8 (QID 0x13B1, 8b)
                help: TjMax Offset. Specified value here is clipped by pCode (125 - TjMax Offset) to support TjMax in the range of
                help: 62 to 115 deg Celsius. Uses Mailbox MSR 0x37, command 0x01. Range 10 - 63, set back to 0 for default if not
                help: in the range. 0 = No offset.
            ▸ 
            • Fast Throttle Threshold  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x395 (QID 0x13BB, 8b)
                help: Fast Throttle Threshold. Specified value for max allowed temperature when cores throttle. Support Fast
                help: Throttle Threshold in the range of 63 to 115 deg Celsius, set back to 0 for default if not in the range. 0 =
                help: Hardware Default.
            ▸ 
            ▸ Thermal Velocity Boost (TVB)
            • Thermal Velocity Boost  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2E3 (QID 0x354, 8b)
                help: This service controls Core frequency reduction caused by high package temperatures for processors that
                help: implement the Intel Thermal Velocity Boost (TVB) feature. It is required to be disabled for supporting
                help: overclocking at frequencies higher than the default max turbo frequency. Default is disabled. Uses
                help: Overclocking Mailbox command 0x18/0x19.
            • Down Bins (delta) for Temperature Threshold 0  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x34E (QID 0x355, 8b)
                help: When running above Temperature Threshold 0, the ratio will be clipped to (Max_Ratio-This Down Bins value)
            • TVB Temperature Threshold 0 (degrees C)  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x34F (QID 0x356, 8b)
                help: Running ABOVE this temperature will clip delta Down Bins for Threshold 0 from the resolved OC Ratio
            • TVB Temperature Threshold 1 (degrees C)  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x350 (QID 0x357, 8b)
                help: Running ABOVE this temperature will clip delta Down Bins for Threshold 1 from the resolved OC Ratio
            • Down Bins (delta) for Temperature Threshold 1  [numeric]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x351 (QID 0x358, 8b)
                help: When running above Temperature Threshold 1, the ratio will be clipped to (Max_Ratio-This Down Bins value)
            • TVB Voltage Optimizations  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x2E4 (QID 0x359, 8b)
                help: This service controls thermal based voltage optimizations for processors that implement the Intel Thermal
                help: Velocity Boost (TVB) feature. Default is enabled. Uses Overclocking Mailbox command 0x18/0x19.
            • Enhanced Thermal Velocity Boost  [oneof]
                meta: CpuSetup @ 0x378 (QID 0x35A, 8b)
                help: If enabled the user will be clipped when the temperatures reach the default threshold on supported products. 
                help: Recommended to disable for overclocking.
            • Expected CPU Freq  [oneof]
                meta: Setup @ 0x7 (QID 0x101A, 8b)
                help: Expected Processor frequency calculated using Bus ratio and Bus speed.

        Memory Overclocking Menu  (0x101C)
            ▸ 
            • Realtime Memory Timing  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x274 (QID 0x47E, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable realtime memory timings. When enabled, the system will allow performing realtime memory timing
                help: changes after MRC_DONE.
            • Memory profile  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x18D (QID 0x112B, 8b)
                help: Select DIMM timing profile. The below values start with the currently running values and don't auto populate.
            • Dynamic Memory Boost  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x41A (QID 0x47F, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Dynamic Memory Boost feature, Allows automatic switching between default SPD Profile frequency
                help: and selected XMP profile frequency. Only valid if an XMP Profile is selected.
            • Realtime Memory Frequency  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x42D (QID 0x480, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable Realtime Memory Frequency feature, Allows manual switching in runtime between default SPD
                help: Profile frequency and selected XMP profile frequency. Only valid if an XMP Profile is selected.
            • OCSafeMode  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x478 (QID 0x481, 8b)
                help: OverClocking Safe Mode: When enabled, memory training uses less aggressive timing and algoes
            • Memory Reference Clock  [oneof]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x5 (QID 0x482, 8b)
                help: 100MHz, 133MHz.
            • Memory Ratio  [numeric]
                meta: SaSetup @ 0x6 (QID 0x483, 8b)
                help: 0 = Auto, or the frequency will equal ratio times reference clock. Set to 0 (Auto) to recalculate memory
                help: timings listed below.

            PCIe EQ settings  (0x1130)
                • PCIe EQ method  [oneof]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x3BD (QID 0x95A, 8b)
                    help: Choose PCIe EQ method
                • PCIe EQ mode  [oneof]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x3BE (QID 0x95B, 8b)
                    help: Choose EQ mode. Preset mode - root port will use presets during EQ process, Coefficient mode - root port will
                    help: use coefficients during EQ process
                • EQ PH1 downstream port transmitter preset  [numeric]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x3C0 (QID 0x95C, 8b)
                    help: Choose the value of the preset that will be used during phase 1 of the equalization
                • EQ PH1 upstream port transmitter preset  [numeric]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x3C1 (QID 0x95D, 8b)
                    help: Choose the value of the preset that will be used during phase 1 of the equalization
                • EQ Phase 2 local transmitter override preset  [numeric]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x3E2 (QID 0x95F, 8b)
                    help: Select preset which will be used during phase 2 of the PCIe EQ process
                • Number of presets or coefficients used during phase 3  [numeric]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x3C2 (QID 0x960, 8b)
                    help: Select how many presets or coefficients will be used during phase 3 of EQ. Please not that you have to set
                    help: all of the list entries to valid values. The interpretation of this field depends on PCIe EQ mode
                • Preset 0  [numeric]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x3D7 (QID 0x961, 8b)

            PCI Express Root Port 1  (0x157C)
                • PCI Express Root Port 1  [oneof]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0xFF (QID 0x980, 8b)
                    help: Control the PCI Express Root Port.
                • Connection Type  [oneof]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x367 (QID 0x981, 8b)
                    help: Built-In: a built-in device is connected to this rootport. SlotImplemented bit will be clear. Slot: this
                    help: rootport connects to user-accessible slot. SlotImplemented bit will be set.

        USB Configuration  (0x1031)
            ▸ USB Configuration
            ▸ 
            • xDCI Support  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x47 (QID 0x8DC, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable xDCI (USB OTG Device).
            ▸ 
            • USB PDO Programming  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x42 (QID 0x8DD, 8b)
                help: Select 'Enabled' if Port Disable Override functionality is used.
            • USB Overcurrent  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x43 (QID 0x8DE, 8b)
                help: Select 'Disabled' for pin-based debug. If pin-based debug is enabled but USB overcurrent is not disabled, USB
                help: DbC does not work.
            • USB Overcurrent Lock  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x44 (QID 0x8DF, 8b)
                help: Select 'Enabled' if Overcurrent functionality is used. Enabling this will make xHCI controller consume the
                help: Overcurrent mapping data
            • USB Audio Offload  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x783 (QID 0x8E0, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable USB Audio Offload functionality
            • Enable HSII on xHCI  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x7F4 (QID 0x8E1, 8b)
                help: Enable/Disable HSII feature. It may lead to increased power consumption.
            ▸ 
            • USB Port Disable Override  [oneof]

        THC Configuration  (0x152E)
            ▸ Touch Host Controller Configuration Settings
            ▸ 
            • THC Port Configuration  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x518 (QID 0xD08, 8b)
                help: Assign Port to THC
            • Port Clock  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x519 (QID 0xD09, 8b)
                help: SPI_DFX_CLK_EN BIT set to 0 or 1
            • Active LTR  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x794 (QID 0xD0A, 32b)
                help: Expose Active LTR data through ACPI _DSM for OS driver to configure
            • Idle LTR  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x79C (QID 0xD0B, 32b)
                help: Expose Idle LTR data through ACPI _DSM for OS driver to configure
            • HID Over SPI Limit Packet Size  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x7A4 (QID 0xD0C, 32b)
                help: When set, limits SPI read & write packet size to 64B. Otherwise, THC uses Max Soc packet size for SPI Read
                help: and Write  0 - Max Soc Packet Size  1 - 64 Bytes
            • Hid Over Spi Performance Limitation  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x7AC (QID 0xD0D, 32b)
                help: Minimum amount of delay the driver must wait between end of write operation and begin of read operation. This
                help: value shall be in 10us multiples  0-Disabled  1-65535 (0xFFFF) up to 655350us
            • Wake On Touch  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x51A (QID 0xD0E, 8b)
                help: Based on this setting vGPIO for given THC will be in native mode, and additional _CRS for wake will be
                help: exposed in ACPI
            • THC Mode  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x792 (QID 0xD0F, 8b)
                help: Switch between Intel THC protcol and Industry standard HID Over SPI protocol.
            • Connection Speed  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x7BC (QID 0xD10, 32b)
                help: HID Over SPI Connection Speed in Hz
            • Flags  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x7B4 (QID 0xD11, 32b)
                help: HID Over SPI Flags
            • Input Report Body Address  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x7C4 (QID 0xD12, 32b)
                help: HID Over SPI Input Report Body Address
            • Input Report Header Address  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x7CC (QID 0xD13, 32b)
                help: HID Over SPI Input Report Header Address

                help: HID Over SPI Output Report Address

                meta: PchSetup @ 0x7E4 (QID 0xD15, 32b)

            • Read Opcode  [numeric]

            ▸ THC is not available

        SerialIo Configuration  (0x7A)
            ▸ SerialIo Configuration
            • I2C0 Controller  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x705 (QID 0xD83, 8b)
                help: Enables/Disables SerialIo Controller If given device is Function 0 PSF disabling is skipped. PSF default will
                help: remain and device PCI CFG Space will still be visible. This is needed to allow PCI enumerator access
                help: functions above 0 in a multifunction device. The following devices depend on each other: I2C0 and I2C1,2,3
                help: UART0 and UART1,SPI0,1 UART2 and I2C4,5   UART 0 (00:30:00) cannot be disabled when: 1. Child device is
                help: enabled like CNVi Bluetooth (\_SB.PC00.UA00.BTH0)  UART 0 (00:30:00) cannot be enabled when: 1. I2S Audio
                help: codec is enabled (\_SB.PC00.I2C0.HDAC)

        Pch Thermal Throttling Control  (0x13BE)
            • Thermal Throttling Level  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x717 (QID 0xE55, 8b)
                help: Determine if use Intel suggested setting
            • T0 Level  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x718 (QID 0xE59, 16b)
                help: If Trip Point Temperature <= T0Level, the system is in T0 state
            • T1 Level  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x71A (QID 0xE5A, 16b)
                help: If T1Level >= Trip Point Temperature > T0Level, the system is in T1 state
            • T2 Level  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x71C (QID 0xE5B, 16b)
                help: If T2Level >= Trip Point Temperature > T1Level, the system is in T2 state
            • DMI Thermal Setting  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x721 (QID 0xE5C, 8b)
                help: Determine if use Intel suggested setting
            • Thermal Sensor 0 Width  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x722 (QID 0xE5E, 8b)
                help: Determine the DMI Link Width when the output from the Thermal Sensor is T0
            • Thermal Sensor 1 Width  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x723 (QID 0xE5F, 8b)
                help: Determine the DMI Link Width when the output from the Thermal Sensor is T1
            • Thermal Sensor 2 Width  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x724 (QID 0xE60, 8b)
                help: Determine the DMI Link Width when the output from the Thermal Sensor is T2
            • Thermal Sensor 3 Width  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x725 (QID 0xE61, 8b)
                help: Determine the DMI Link Width when the output from the Thermal Sensor is T3
            • SATA Thermal Setting  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x727 (QID 0xE62, 8b)
                help: Determine if use Intel suggested setting
            ▸ Port 0
            • T1 Multipler  [oneof]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x728 (QID 0xE63, 8b)
                help: Determine the value of SATA Port T1 Multipler
            • T2 Multipler  [oneof]

        FIVR Configuration  (0x13E1)
            • Off to High Current Mode  [numeric]
                meta: PchSetup @ 0x76B (QID 0xE83, 16b)
                help: Transition time in microseconds from Off (0V) to High Current Mode Voltage. This field has 1us resolution. 0
                help: = Transition to 0V is disabled. The value must be greater than or equal to VccST board FET ramp time
        PMC Configuration  (0x13E2)
            PMC ADR Configuration  (0x13E3)
                • ADR enable  [oneof]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x77F (QID 0xE85, 8b)
                    help: Enable asynchronous DRAM refresh
                • Host Partition Reset ADR Enable  [oneof]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x782 (QID 0xE86, 8b)
                    help: Enables/Disables ADR on Host Partition Reset
                • ADR timer 1 expire time  [numeric]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x780 (QID 0xE87, 8b)
                    help: Type desired ADR timer expire time, valid values - <1, 256>. Entered time is scaled by ADR timer time unit.
                • ADR timer 1 time unit  [oneof]
                    meta: PchSetup @ 0x781 (QID 0xE88, 8b)
                    help: Select ADR timer time unit.

        Anti-Rollback SVN Configuration  (0x1044)
            • Automatic HW-Enforced Anti-Rollback SVN  [oneof]
                meta: MeSetup @ 0x28 (QID 0xEA8, 8b)
                help: When enabled, hardware-enforced Anti-Rollback mechanism is automatically activated: once ME FW was
                help: successfully run on a platform, FW with lower ARB-SVN will be blocked from execution
            • Set HW-Enforced Anti-Rollback for Current SVN  [oneof]
                meta: MeSetupStorage @ 0xB (QID 0xEA9, 8b)
                help: Enable hardware-enforced Anti-Rollback mechanism for current ARB-SVN value. FW with lower ARB-SVN will be
                help: blocked from execution. The value will be restored to disable after the command is sent.
